{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Jcnm7eFqWvKdpCaBHYNw9d","type":"tool_result","content":"./README.md\n./data/appointments.sqlite\n./notes/ops_notes.md\n./mlops/__init__.py\n./mlops/monitor.py\n./mlops/data.py\n./mlops/metrics.py\n./mlops/scoring.py\n./mlops/__main__.py\n./mlops/cli.py\n./docs/model_risk_standard.md\n./docs/reminder_programme_sop.md\n./docs/table_dictionary.md\n./reports/vendor_monitoring_report.md\n./docs/outputs/readout_contract.md\n# MRM-04 — Model risk management standard (extract)\n\nHalcyon Health Partners. Issue 3. Applies to all models used to direct patient contact or clinical capacity.\n\n## 4. Ongoing monitoring\n\n### 4.1 Population\n\nPerformance monitoring shall be computed on a population whose outcomes are **not influenced by the model's own\nuse**. Where a model's output drives an intervention, the intervention changes the outcome being predicted, and\na metric computed on the intervened population measures the intervention as well as the model.\n\nIt is the model owner's responsibility to identify a suitable population from the deployment design and to\nrecord which population was used in every monitoring submission.\n\n### 4.2 Scoring basis\n\nMonitoring shall state the scoring basis. Where a model's features are rebuilt after deployment, the figure\ncomputed on the rebuilt features is not the figure the model achieved in service, and both shall be reported.\n\n### 4.3 Retention floor\n\nA model may be **retained** only while its discrimination on a population meeting §4.1, computed on the scores\nit produced in service (§4.2), is\n\n* within **0.04 AUC** of the validation figure recorded in the model registry, **and**\n* not below **0.70 AUC**.\n\nThe lower of the two gives the retention floor.\n\n### 4.4 Where the floor is breached\n\nWhere the floor is breached the model owner shall determine the cause and act in the following order.\n\n1. **Feed integrity first.** If more than **5 %** of scored records in the window were served a feature value\n   that does not agree with the source record or with the append-only event history, the finding is a defect in\n   the feature feed. Where rescoring from the source restores discrimination above the floor, the required\n   action is `remediate_feature_pipeline`. A model shall not be retrained or replaced to compensate for a\n   defective feed.\n2. **Candidate comparison.** Otherwise, if a candidate model's discrimination on the same population exceeds the\n   incumbent's by more than **0.02 AUC**, the required action is `replace_with_v4` (or the candidate under\n   consideration).\n3. **Otherwise** the required action is `retrain_on_recent_data`.\n\nA candidate trained on data from a period in which the incumbent's output directed an intervention shall be\nevaluated on a population meeting §4.1 before any comparison under (2) is relied upon.\n\n## 5. Records\n\nEvery monitoring submission shall record the population, the scoring basis, the validation figure it is\ncompared against, and the action taken.\n# SOP-118 — Outpatient reminder-call programme\n\n## 1. Purpose\n\nTo reduce did-not-attend rates in outpatient clinics by telephoning patients at higher risk of not attending.\n\n## 2. Targeting\n\nPatients are selected by the risk score recorded in `model_scores` for the model named in\n`policy_config` under `programme.score_model`. The share of the score distribution called, the start week and\nthe clinic ramp order are all held in `policy_config` and are not to be changed without Clinical Operations\nsign-off.\n\nThe calling threshold was fixed at programme start from the score distribution observed before the programme\nbegan, and has not been changed since.\n\n## 3. Rollout\n\nClinics joined in `programme.ramp_group` order, one group per week, over `programme.ramp_weeks` weeks.\n\nA set of clinics listed under `programme.excluded_clinics` was determined at programme start and is outside the\nprogramme. The selection was drawn stratified by `clinics.size_band`. Requests to bring an excluded clinic into\nthe programme go to Clinical Operations; none have been made.\n\n## 4. Calling\n\nBookings selected for calling appear in `reminder_calls` with the call outcome. `REACHED` means the patient was\nspoken to. `NO_ANSWER` and `VOICEMAIL` mean no contact was made; no message is left describing the appointment.\n\n## 5. Reporting\n\nProgramme reporting is the responsibility of Clinical Operations. Model performance reporting is separate and\nis governed by MRM-04.\n# data/appointments.sqlite — table dictionary\n\n## appointments\nOne row per booked outpatient appointment, 52 weeks. Patient and appointment attributes are as held on the\n**source record**.\n\n| column | meaning |\n|---|---|\n| appt_id, clinic_id, patient_id | keys |\n| booked_on, appointment_on, week | booking date, appointment date, ISO production week |\n| appointment_type, lead_time_days, age_band, deprivation_decile, distance_km, interpreter_required | appointment and patient attributes |\n| referral_source | `GP`, or the partnership source that began referring in month 4 |\n| prior_no_show_count | the no-show count held in the **current** feature table; the window it covers is in `policy_config` under `feature_store.prior_no_show_window_days` |\n| attended, no_show | the realised outcome |\n\n## feature_snapshots\nWhat the scorer was **given** at booking, one row per appointment. This is the serving vintage.\n\n## attendance_events\nAppend-only attendance history, including events that predate the extract. `appt_id` is null for events that\npredate the extract.\n\n## model_scores\n`(appt_id, model_version, score)`. For `noshow-v3.1` these are the scores produced at booking. For\n`noshow-v4.0` they are a batch rescore of every appointment.\n\n## model_registry\nOne row per model version, with the validation figure, the feature window the model was defined on, and the\npublished coefficients for `noshow-v3.1`.\n\n## reminder_calls\nOne row per booking selected for calling, with the call outcome.\n\n## policy_config\nKey/value configuration of the reminder programme and the feature store, with effective dates.\n\n## monitoring_metrics\nThe vendor's dashboard series: weekly AUC and no-show rate on the population it monitors.\n\n## clinics\nClinic reference data, including `size_band` and `ramp_group`.\n# Required outputs\n\n`mlops.monitor` must write two files into `out/` when run as\n\n    python -m mlops monitor --db data/appointments.sqlite --out out\n\n## out/readout.json\n\n| field | type | meaning |\n|---|---|---|\n| `model_version` | string | the model under review |\n| `window_weeks` | [int, int] | the production weeks the readout covers, inclusive |\n| `monitored_auc` | number | the AUC the current monitoring series reports for that window |\n| `validation_auc` | number | the model registry's validation figure |\n| `retention_floor_auc` | number | the floor implied by MRM-04 §4.3 |\n| `evaluation_population` | object | `clinic_ids` (list) and `n_appointments` (int) for the population you evaluated on under MRM-04 §4.1 |\n| `auc_by_scoring` | object | the AUC on that population under each scoring basis available (see below) |\n| `feed_defect_share_pct` | number | share of scored records in the window whose served feature values disagree with the source record or the event history |\n| `programme_effect_pp` | number | effect of the reminder programme on the no-show rate, in percentage points, in the score band the programme acts on, stratified by clinic size band |\n| `attribution_auc` | object | how the gap between `validation_auc` and `monitored_auc` divides between causes, in AUC points |\n| `decision` | string | the action MRM-04 §4.3-§4.4 requires |\n\n### `auc_by_scoring`\n\nExactly these four keys:\n\n    as_served · record_features_asof_window · feature_store_current · candidate_v4\n\n`as_served` is the scores in `model_scores` for the model under review. `record_features_asof_window` is the\nmodel rescored from its published coefficients over the source record and the event history, on the feature\nwindow the registry records for it. `feature_store_current` is the model rescored over the feature store as it\nnow stands. `candidate_v4` is the candidate's scores.\n\n### `attribution_auc`\n\nExactly these five keys, summing to `validation_auc - monitored_auc` within 0.01:\n\n    population_drift · feature_feed_defect · feature_vintage · policy_feedback · other\n\nAny key may be zero or negative. Do not add or rename keys.\n\n### `decision`\n\nOne of `retain_model`, `retrain_on_recent_data`, `replace_with_v4`, `remediate_feature_pipeline`.\n\n## out/evaluation_population.csv\n\nHeader `clinic_id,n_appointments,no_show_rate,auc_as_served`, one row per clinic in the population you\nevaluated on.\n\n## Rules\n\n* Do not modify `data/appointments.sqlite`.\n* The command must be re-runnable and produce the same outputs for the same database.\n* AUCs to at least three decimals; rates and effects in percentage points.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d4f0f95-51f6-466d-a766-df9a6509f625","uuid":"1393add6-6be2-455f-8c34-77ccff706f33","timestamp":"2026-10-01T06:56:21.683Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"./README.md\n./data/appointments.sqlite\n./notes/ops_notes.md\n./mlops/__init__.py\n./mlops/monitor.py\n./mlops/data.py\n./mlops/metrics.py\n./mlops/scoring.py\n./mlops/__main__.py\n./mlops/cli.py\n./docs/model_risk_standard.md\n./docs/reminder_programme_sop.md\n./docs/table_dictionary.md\n./reports/vendor_monitoring_report.md\n./docs/outputs/readout_contract.md\n# MRM-04 — Model risk management standard (extract)\n\nHalcyon Health Partners. Do not modify it.\n# Clinical Operations / data platform — running notes (extracts)\n\n**w27** Reminder programme live. Calling threshold fixed from the pre-programme score distribution. Ramp by\nclinic group over six weeks as per SOP-118. Excluded clinics set at kick-off and logged in the policy config.\n\n**w29** Early read from Clinical Ops: DNA down in the clinics that have joined. They are pleased.\n\n**w33** Platform change: the no-show count in the feature store was widened from the rolling twelve months to a\nlonger window, to make the feature more stable for low-volume clinics. Change request CR-2291. The model was\nnot re-fitted; nobody flagged that the registry records the window the model was defined on.\n\n**w34** Vendor asked for a feature-store extract for retraining. Sent.\n\n**w38** New partnership referral source now a material share of first appointments. Lead times on those are\nlonger. Clinical Ops want a decision before the winter capacity plan.\n# noshow-v3.1 — monitoring review and retrain proposal\nPrepared by Calder Analytics (model vendor) for Halcyon Health Partners. 2026-07-02.\n\n## Summary\n\n`noshow-v3.1` has lost discrimination since deployment. Against a validation figure of **0.774 AUC**, the\nmonitoring series for the most recent twelve weeks stands at **0.702 AUC**, a fall of 7.2 points.\n\nWe attribute the fall to data drift and recommend adopting the retrained `noshow-v4.0`.\n\n## Evidence\n\n**Feature drift.** Population Stability Index against the training period exceeds the usual 0.2 action\nthreshold on four features, and the referral mix has changed materially since month 4 with the arrival of the\npartnership source. Lead times on partnership referrals run roughly eight days longer.\n\n**Monotone decline.** The weekly AUC series in `monitoring_metrics` declines through the period. The decline is\nnot confined to any one clinic group.\n\n**Temporal validation.** Training on the earlier part of the period and testing on the later part reproduces\nthe decline, which rules out an evaluation artefact.\n\n**Retrain restores performance.** `noshow-v4.0`, fitted on data from the programme period onward, achieves\n0.79 AUC when validated on a recent holdout drawn from the clinics the programme covers. Scores for every\nappointment in the extract are supplied in `model_scores` so that Halcyon can reproduce this.\n\n## Recommendation\n\nAdopt `noshow-v4.0`. We further recommend moving to a quarterly retrain cadence, since the drift we observe\nappears to be ongoing rather than a one-off shift.\n\n## Notes\n\nThe monitoring series is computed from the feature store as it currently stands, which we rebuilt in week 33\nwhen the no-show count window was widened. We have not recomputed the series on the earlier feature definition.\n\"\"\"Halcyon Health Partners model monitoring.\"\"\"\n__all__ = [\"cli\", \"data\", \"metrics\", \"monitor\"]\nimport sys\n\nfrom mlops.cli import main\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))\nimport argparse\n\nfrom mlops.monitor import write_readout\n\n\ndef main(argv=None):\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"mlops\", description=\"model monitoring readout\")\n    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest=\"cmd\", required=True)\n    m = sub.add_parser(\"monitor\", help=\"produce the monitoring readout\")\n    m.add_argument(\"--db\", default=\"data/appointments.sqlite\")\n    m.add_argument(\"--out\", default=\"out\")\n    a = ap.parse_args(argv)\n    if a.cmd == \"monitor\":\n        write_readout(a.db, a.out)\n    return 0\n\"\"\"Database access for the monitoring readout.\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\n\nWINDOW_WEEKS = 12\nMODEL = \"noshow-v3.1\"\nCANDIDATE = \"noshow-v4.0\"\n\n\ndef connect(db_path):\n    con = sqlite3.connect(f\"file:{db_path}?mode=ro\", uri=True)\n    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return con\n\n\ndef last_week(con):\n    return con.execute(\"SELECT MAX(week) AS w FROM appointments\").fetchone()[\"w\"]\n\n\ndef window(con):\n    lw = last_week(con)\n    return (lw - WINDOW_WEEKS + 1, lw)\n\n\ndef appointments(con, lo, hi):\n    return con.execute(\n        \"SELECT a.*, f.prior_no_shows_12m AS served_prior, f.deprivation_decile AS served_decile, \"\n        \"       f.age_band AS served_age, f.appointment_type AS served_type, \"\n        \"       s.score AS score_v31, v.score AS score_v4 \"\n        \"FROM appointments a \"\n        \"JOIN feature_snapshots f ON f.appt_id = a.appt_id \"\n        \"LEFT JOIN model_scores s ON s.appt_id = a.appt_id AND s.model_version = ? \"\n        \"LEFT JOIN model_scores v ON v.appt_id = a.appt_id AND v.model_version = ? \"\n        \"WHERE a.week BETWEEN ? AND ?\", (MODEL, CANDIDATE, lo, hi)).fetchall()\n\n\ndef registry(con, version=MODEL):\n    return con.execute(\"SELECT * FROM model_registry WHERE model_version = ?\", (version,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef coefficients(con, version=MODEL):\n    row = registry(con, version)\n    return json.loads(row[\"coefficients_json\"]) if row and row[\"coefficients_json\"] else None\n\n\ndef policy(con):\n    return {r[\"key\"]: r[\"value\"] for r in con.execute(\"SELECT key, value FROM policy_config\")}\n\n\ndef monitored_auc(con, lo, hi):\n    rows = con.execute(\n        \"SELECT value FROM monitoring_metrics WHERE metric='auc' AND week BETWEEN ? AND ?\", (lo, hi)).fetchall()\n    return sum(r[\"value\"] for r in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None\n\"\"\"Discrimination and rate helpers.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef auc(scores, labels):\n    pairs = sorted(zip(scores, labels))\n    n = len(pairs)\n    ranks = [0.0] * n\n    i = 0\n    while i < n:\n        j = i\n        while j + 1 < n and pairs[j + 1][0] == pairs[i][0]:\n            j += 1\n        r = (i + j) / 2.0 + 1.0\n        for k in range(i, j + 1):\n            ranks[k] = r\n        i = j + 1\n    pos = sum(1 for _, y in pairs if y == 1)\n    neg = n - pos\n    if pos == 0 or neg == 0:\n        return None\n    s = sum(r for r, (_, y) in zip(ranks, pairs) if y == 1)\n    return (s - pos * (pos + 1) / 2.0) / (pos * neg)\n\n\ndef no_show_rate_pct(rows):\n    return 100.0 * sum(r[\"no_show\"] for r in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None\n\"\"\"Monitoring readout for noshow-v3.1.\n\nThe AUC is taken over the clinics the reminder programme covers, which is the population the dashboard has\nalways reported on, using the feature store as it currently stands.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\n\nfrom mlops import data, metrics, scoring\n\n\ndef write_readout(db_path, out_dir):\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    con = data.connect(db_path)\n    try:\n        lo, hi = data.window(con)\n        rows = data.appointments(con, lo, hi)\n        reg = data.registry(con)\n        coef = data.coefficients(con)\n        cur = {r[\"appt_id\"]: scoring.score_feature_store_current(coef, r) for r in rows}\n        monitored = metrics.auc([cur[r[\"appt_id\"]] for r in rows], [r[\"no_show\"] for r in rows])\n        validation = reg[\"validation_auc\"]\n        readout = {\n            \"model_version\": data.MODEL,\n            \"window_weeks\": [lo, hi],\n            \"monitored_auc\": round(monitored, 4),\n            \"validation_auc\": validation,\n            \"auc_by_scoring\": {\n                \"as_served\": round(monitored, 4),\n                \"candidate_v4\": round(metrics.auc([r[\"score_v4\"] for r in rows],\n                                                  [r[\"no_show\"] for r in rows]), 4),\n            },\n            \"attribution_auc\": {\n                \"population_drift\": round(validation - r[\"clinic_id\"] in programme_clinics]\n        control = [r for r in band if r[\"size_band\"] == size and r[\"clinic_id\"] in control_clinics]\n        if treated and control:\n            total += len(treated) * (metrics.no_show_rate_pct(treated) - metrics.no_show_rate_pct(control))\n            weight += len(treated)\n    return total / weight if weight else None\n\n\ndef decide(as_served, floor, defect_pct, rescored_from_source, candidate):\n    \"\"\"MRM-04 §4.3-§4.4.\"\"\"\n    if as_served >= floor:\n        return \"retain_model\"\n    if defect_pct > FEED_DEFECT_PCT and rescored_from_source >= floor:\n        return \"remediate_feature_pipeline\"\n    if candidate - as_served > CANDIDATE_MARGIN:\n        return \"replace_with_v4\"\n    return \"retrain_on_recent_data\"\n\n\ndef write_readout(db_path, out_dir):\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    con = data.connect(db_path)\n    try:\n        lo, hi = data.window(con)\n        pol = data.policy(con)\n        reg = data.registry(con)\n        coef = data.coefficients(con)\n        events = data.no_show_events(con)\n        rows = [dict(r) for r in data.appointments(con, lo, hi)]\n        start_week = int(pol[\"programme.start_week\"])\n        threshold = _quantile(data.served_scores(con, 1, start_week - 1),\n                              1.0 - float(pol[\"programme.score_percentile\"]))\n    finally:\n        con.close()\n\n    window_days = reg[\"feature_window_days\"]\n    defects = 0\n    for r in rows:\n        prior = scoring.prior_no_shows(events, r[\"patient_id\"], r[\"booked_on\"], window_days)\n        defects += _feed_defect(r, prior)\n        r[\"score_record\"] = scoring.score_record_asof(coef, r, prior)\n        r[\"score_current\"] = scoring.score_feature_store_current(coef, r)\n    feed_defect_pct = 100.0 * defects / len(rows)\n\n    # §4.1: excluded clinics, and only those with no score-driven calls.\n    excluded = {c.strip() for c in pol[\"programme.excluded_clinics\"].split(\",\") if c.strip()}\n    called = {r[\"clinic_id\"] for r in rows if r[\"call_outcome\"]}\n    control = sorted(excluded - called)\n    programme = {r[\"clinic_id\"] for r in rows} - excluded\n    pop = [r for r in rows if r[\"clinic_id\"] in control]\n\n    validation = reg[\"validation_auc\"]\n    floor = max(validation - AUC_TOLERANCE, AUC_ABSOLUTE_FLOOR)\n    monitored = data.monitored_auc(con_path_free := None, lo, hi) if False else None  # placeholder, set below\n    con = data.connect(db_path)\n    try:\n        monitored = data.monitored_auc(con, lo, hi)\n    finally:\n        con.close()\n\n    auc_by_scoring = {\n        \"as_served\": _auc(pop, \"score_v31\"),\n        \"record_features_asof_window\": _auc(pop, \"score_record\"),\n        \"feature_store_current\": _auc(pop, \"score_current\"),\n        \"candidate_v4\": _auc(pop, \"score_v4\"),\n    }\n\n    # Attribution, stepping from the validation figure to the monitored series:\n    #   validation -> as served on the §4.1 population in the window          population_drift\n    #   -> as served on all clinics (adds the clinics the programme calls)    policy_feedback\n    #   -> served-feature rescore vs as-served basis on all clinics            (other)\n    #   -> rescore on served features vs on source record + event history     feature_feed_defect\n    #   -> rescore on record features vs on the current feature store         feature_vintage\n    #   -> pooled current-store AUC vs the dashboard's weekly series           (other)\n    all_served = _auc(rows, \"score_v31\")\n    for r in rows:\n        r[\"score_served_features\"] = scoring.score_served_features(coef, r)\n    all_served_feat = _auc(rows, \"score_served_features\")\n    all_record = _auc(rows, \"score_record\")\n    all_current = _auc(rows, \"score_current\")\n    attribution = {\n        \"population_drift\": validation - auc_by_scoring[\"as_served\"],\n        \"policy_feedback\": auc_by_scoring[\"as_served\"] - all_served,\n        \"feature_feed_defect\": all_record - all_served_feat,\n        \"feature_vintage\": all_record - all_current,\n        \"other\": 0.0,\n    }\n    gap = validation - monitored\n    attribution[\"other\"] = gap - sum(attribution.values())\n\n    decision = decide(auc_by_scoring[\"as_served\"], floor, feed_defect_pct,\n                      auc_by_scoring[\"record_features_asof_window\"], auc_by_scoring[\"candidate_v4\"])\n\n    readout = {\n        \"model_version\": data.MODEL,\n        \"window_weeks\": [lo, hi],\n        \"monitored_auc\": round(monitored, 4),\n        \"validation_auc\": validation,\n        \"retention_floor_auc\": round(floor, 4),\n        \"evaluation_population\": {\"clinic_ids\": control, \"n_appointments\": len(pop)},\n        \"auc_by_scoring\": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in auc_by_scoring.items()},\n        \"feed_defect_share_pct\": round(feed_defect_pct, 4),\n        \"programme_effect_pp\": round(programme_effect_pp(rows, programme, set(control), threshold), 2),\n        \"attribution_auc\": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in attribution.items()},\n        \"decision\": decision,\n    }\n    with open(os.path.join(out_dir, \"readout.json\"), \"w\") as fh:\n        json.dump(readout, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True)\n        fh.write(\"\\n\")\n\n    with open(os.path.join(out_dir, Window: production weeks 41–52.\nReproduce with `python -m mlops monitor --db data/appointments.sqlite --out out` (figures below are from\n`out/readout.json` and `out/evaluation_population.csv`).\n\n## Decision: `retain_model`\n\nOn a population whose outcomes are not influenced by the model's use, using the scores it produced in service,\nnoshow-v3.1 achieves **0.790 AUC**. The retention floor is **0.730**, so the model passes. The vendor's\n0.71 comes from (a) clinics where the model's own score decides who gets a reminder call and (b) a feature\ntable rebuilt after deployment. It is not evidence that the model has degraded. `noshow-v4.0` scores **0.764**\non the same population, lower than the incumbent, so the case for replacing v3.1 does not hold.\n\n## §5 record\n\n| item | value |\n|---|---|\n| Population (§4.1) | Clinics excluded from the reminder programme at kick-off: CL-001, CL-005, CL-012, CL-028, CL-038 (stratified by size band, per `policy_config`). Verified: no reminder calls in these clinics. 4,136 appointments in w41–52. |\n| Scoring basis (§4.2) | `as_served`: the noshow-v3.1 scores recorded at booking in `model_scores` |\n| Validation figure | **0.7704** (`model_registry`). The vendor report gives 0.774, which is not the registry figure. |\n| Retention floor (§4.3) | max(0.7704 − 0.04, 0.70) = **0.7304**. Both conditions must hold, so the binding floor is the higher one. The standard's sentence \"the lower of the two gives the floor\" reads oddly. Under either reading the model passes. |\n| Action | `retain_model` |\n\n## AUC on the evaluation population, by scoring basis\n\n| basis | AUC |\n|---|---|\n| as_served (governs §4.3) | **0.7904** |\n| record_features_asof_window (registry coefficients, source record + 365-day count from the event history) | 0.7815 |\n| feature_store_current (no-show count over the 730-day window in force since w33, CR-2291) | 0.7765 |\n| candidate_v4 | 0.7641 |\n\nPer-clinic as-served AUC runs from 0.768 to 0.806, so every one of the five clinics is above the floor.\n\n## Feed integrity (§4.4.1)\n\nThe served values in `feature_snapshots` match the source record on every attribute. They match the 365-day\ndid-not-attend count from `attendance_events` on all but 2 of the 31,200 scored records in the window\n(**0.0064 %**). In both mismatches the did-not-attend event was dated 1–3 days before booking and probably had\nnot been posted yet at scoring time. This is far below the 5 % threshold, so there is no feed defect.\n\nThe w33 widening of the window (365 → 730 days) changed the **current feature table only**. Serving kept the\n365-day definition the model was fitted on. The change affects the vendor's monitoring series, not the scores\npatients received.\n\n## Decomposition of the gap (0.7704 → 0.7105 = 0.060 AUC)\n\n`monitored_auc` (0.7105) is the vendor's `monitoring_metrics` series (all clinics, current feature store)\naveraged over w41–52.\n\n| cause | AUC points | step |\n|---|---|---|\n| population_drift | −0.020 | validation → as-served on the §4.1 population in the window (0.790). Discrimination rose: the partnership referrals (14 % of bookings) have longer lead times and higher no-show rates, which makes them easier to separate. |\n| policy_feedback | +0.069 | adding the clinics the programme calls (as-served, all clinics: 0.722). Calling the top 45 % of scores cut no-shows in exactly the high-score band, so the score now separates outcomes less well. That reflects the programme working, not the model failing. |\n| feature_feed_defect | 0.000 | rescore on served features vs on source record + event history (all clinics) |\n| feature_vintage | +0.009 | rescore on 365-day record features (0.719) vs on the current 730-day store (0.710) |\n| other | +0.002 | the gap between the served scores and the published-coefficient rescore, plus pooled vs weekly-mean aggregation in the series |\n\nThese steps are taken in a fixed order, so the split between causes depends on that order. The total is exact.\n\n## Programme effect\n\nIn the score band the programme calls (as-served score ≥ 0.0824, the 55th percentile of pre-programme scores,\nw1–26), programme clinics have a no-show rate **10.6 pp lower** than excluded clinics in w41–52. This is\nstratified by size band and weighted by programme bookings: SMALL −9.0, MEDIUM −13.7, LARGE −9.0 pp. In the\nprogramme clinics, calls were made exactly to the in-band bookings, with no exceptions either way.\n\n## Why noshow-v4.0 is not adopted\n\n* Under §4.4 the candidate comparison is reached only if the floor is breached. It is not breached.\n* On the §4.1 population v4.0 scores 0.764, which is 0.026 *below* v3.1.\n* v4.0 was trained on programme-period data. Its 0.79 holdout came from programme clinics, where outcomes\n  were shaped by v3.1's calls. MRM-04 §4 bars relying on such a comparison until the candidate has been\n  evaluated on a §4.1 population, which is the result above.\n\n## Caveats and follow-ups\n\n* **Small control population.** There are 5 clinics and 4,136 appointments (about 500 no-shows), so the AUC has\n  a standard error of roughly 0.01. The margin over the floor (0.060) is several times that.\n* **Spill-over.** 60 % of bookings in the excluded clinics belong to patients who were called about\n  appointments elsewhere. Calls are specific to an appointment, so these outcomes were not targeted. Splitting\n  the bookings, AUC is 0.739 for patients never called and 0.754 for patients called elsewhere. Both are above\n  the floor. (Each subgroup is lower than the pooled 0.790 because the two groups differ in risk mix.)\n* **Monitoring series.** The vendor dashboard should be recomputed on as-served scores over the excluded\n  clinics. As it stands it mixes in policy feedback and the w33 feature rebuild.\n* **CR-2291 risk.** The current feature table no longer matches the definition in the registry. Any future\n  path that scores from that table would be a feed defect under §4.4.1, so it should be guarded.\n* **Excluded clinics must stay excluded.** Keeping them out of the programme is what keeps a §4.1 population\n  available. This should be raised with Clinical Operations before any expansion.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":558,"cache_read_input_tokens":64202,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":558,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d4f0f95-51f6-466d-a766-df9a6509f625","uuid":"8fa58fdd-7cad-44f7-a334-2ef441c4e26a","timestamp":"2026-10-01T07:05:46.410Z","request_id":"req_011Cfb6KLen4wR9bhTgbLGsM"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfb6Nby6tCdfaah9AW4je","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The model passes MRM-04, so the decision is **`retain_model`** and we should not adopt `noshow-v4.0`. Measured properly, noshow-v3.1 scores 0.790 AUC against a retention floor of 0.730. The vendor's 0.71 is measured on the wrong population and from the wrong features. The repaired package produces `out/readout.json` and `out/evaluation_population.csv`; two runs gave byte-identical files. The submission write-up is in `reports/mrm04_monitoring_submission.md`.\n\n**What the figure is computed on (§4.1).** The reminder programme calls bookings whose v3.1 score is in the top 45% of the pre-programme distribution. In programme clinics this matches exactly: every in-band booking was called and no other booking was. Outcomes there are therefore shaped by the model's own use. The population that meets §4.1 is the five clinics excluded at kick-off (CL-001, -005, -012, -028, -038), which received no calls: 4,136 appointments in weeks 41–52.\n\n**What it is computed from (§4.2).** The scores actually produced at booking. The vendor's series instead uses the feature table rebuilt in week 33, when the no-show count window was widened from 365 to 730 days (CR-2291). That change never reached serving: the served features still match the 365-day definition the model was fitted on.\n\n| Basis, on the excluded clinics | AUC |\n|---|---|\n| Scores produced at booking (this is the §4.3 test) | **0.790** |\n| Rescored from source records, 365-day count | 0.782 |\n| Rescored from the current 730-day feature table | 0.777 |\n| `noshow-v4.0` | 0.764 |\n\n- **Validation figure and floor:** the registry records 0.7704 (the vendor quotes 0.774, which isn't the registry figure). The floor is the higher of 0.7704 − 0.04 and 0.70, which is 0.7304. MRM-04 says \"the lower of the two gives the floor\", which doesn't fit its \"and\" condition. I used the higher value; the model passes under either reading.\n- **Feed integrity:** 0.0064% of served records disagree with the source (2 of 31,200), far below the 5% trigger. Both are no-show events recorded one to three days before booking, likely not yet posted when the score was made.\n- **Programme effect:** in the score band the programme calls, no-show rates are 10.6 points lower in programme clinics than in excluded ones. By size band: small −9.0, medium −13.7, large −9.0.\n- **Gap breakdown:** the gap from 0.7704 to 0.7105 splits as below. The total is exact, but the split between causes depends on the order the steps are applied in.\n\n| Cause | AUC points |\n|---|---|\n| Population drift (AUC actually rose; the new partnership referrals don't explain it) | −0.020 |\n| Policy feedback (calls cut no-shows among high scorers) | +0.069 |\n| Feature feed defect | 0.000 |\n| Feature vintage (the w33 rebuild) | +0.009 |\n| Other | +0.002 |\n\n- **Why not v4.0:** under §4.4 a candidate is only compared once the floor is breached, and it isn't. On the excluded clinics v4.0 is 0.026 below v3.1. Its 0.79 claim came from programme clinics, whose outcomes v3.1's calls had already shaped.\n\n**Package repairs:**\n- The monitor no longer computes \"as served\" from the rebuilt feature table.\n- It now evaluates on the excluded clinics, not all clinics.\n- It now produces all four scoring bases, the feed-integrity check, the programme effect and the gap breakdown.\n- `replace_with_v4` was hard-coded as the decision; the decision now follows the §4.3–4.4 rules.\n- `scoring.py` can now rebuild the 365-day no-show count from the event history.\n\n**Caveats:**\n- The excluded clinics are a small sample (about 500 no-shows), so AUC is uncertain by roughly ±0.01. The 0.06 margin over the floor is still comfortable.\n- 60% of excluded-clinic bookings are for patients who were called about appointments at other clinics. Split by that, AUC is 0.739 for patients never called and 0.754 for those called elsewhere; both are above the floor.\n- The vendor's dashboard should be recomputed on the scores produced at booking, over the excluded clinics.\n- Nothing should start scoring from the current 730-day feature table: that would be a real feed defect under §4.4.\n- The excluded clinics need to stay out of the programme, because they are the only population that meets §4.1."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":786,"cache_read_input_tokens":68097,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":786,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d4f0f95-51f6-466d-a766-df9a6509f625","uuid":"8c638068-6756-43d8-8bef-00f87f725eb7","timestamp":"2026-10-01T07:06:19.651Z","request_id":"req_011Cfb6Nb72ENByZUCVs9Xtr"}
